using Terraria.Enums;
using Terraria.ID;

namespace Terraria.GameContent;

public static class ShimmerTransforms
{
	public static class RecipeSets
	{
		public static bool[] PostSkeletron;

		public static bool[] PostGolem;
	}

	public static int GetDecraftingRecipeIndex(int type)
	{
		int num = ItemID.Sets.IsCrafted[type];
		if (num < 0)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		if (WorldGen.crimson && ItemID.Sets.IsCraftedCrimson[type] >= 0)
		{
			return ItemID.Sets.IsCraftedCrimson[type];
		}
		if (!WorldGen.crimson && ItemID.Sets.IsCraftedCorruption[type] >= 0)
		{
			return ItemID.Sets.IsCraftedCorruption[type];
		}
		return num;
	}

	public static bool IsItemTransformLocked(int type)
	{
		if (!NPC.downedMoonlord && ItemID.Sets.ShimmerPostMoonlord[type])
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool IsItemDecraftLocked(int type)
	{
		return IsRecipeIndexDecraftLocked(GetDecraftingRecipeIndex(type));
	}

	public static bool IsRecipeIndexDecraftLocked(int recipeIndex)
	{
		if (recipeIndex < 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!NPC.downedBoss3 && RecipeSets.PostSkeletron[recipeIndex])
		{
			return true;
		}
		if (!NPC.downedGolemBoss && RecipeSets.PostGolem[recipeIndex])
		{
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static bool IsItemDecraftableAndIsDecraftUnlocked(Item item)
	{
		if (item == null)
		{
			return false;
		}
		int decraftingRecipeIndex = GetDecraftingRecipeIndex(item.GetShimmerEquivalentType(forDecrafting: true));
		if (IsRecipeIndexDecraftLocked(decraftingRecipeIndex))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (decraftingRecipeIndex < 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return item.stack / Main.recipe[decraftingRecipeIndex].createItem.stack > 0;
	}

	public static void UpdateRecipeSets()
	{
		RecipeSets.PostSkeletron = Utils.MapArray(Main.recipe, (Recipe r) => r.ContainsIngredient(154));
		RecipeSets.PostGolem = Utils.MapArray(Main.recipe, (Recipe r) => r.ContainsIngredient(1101));
	}

	public static int GetTransformToItem(int type)
	{
		int num = ItemID.Sets.ShimmerTransformToItem[type];
		if (num > 0)
		{
			return num;
		}
		if (ContentSamples.ItemsByType[type].createTile == 139)
		{
			return ContentSamples.ItemsByType[type].placeStyle switch
			{
				90 => 5538, 
				89 => 5579, 
				97 => 5638, 
				96 => 5639, 
				_ => 576, 
			};
		}
		if (type == 3461)
		{
			return GetLunarBrickTransformFromMoonPhase(Main.GetMoonPhase());
		}
		return 0;
	}

	private static int GetLunarBrickTransformFromMoonPhase(MoonPhase moonPhase)
	{
		return moonPhase switch
		{
			MoonPhase.QuarterAtRight => 5407, 
			MoonPhase.HalfAtRight => 5405, 
			MoonPhase.ThreeQuartersAtRight => 5404, 
			MoonPhase.Full => 5408,

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-12T15:57:41.180Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Now I understand the vanilla mechanics:

1. `GetShimmerEquivalentType(forDecrafting: true)` → `ItemID.Sets.ShimmerCountsAsItemForDecraft[type]` if != -1, else `ShimmerCountsAsItem[type]`, else type.
2. `GetDecraftingRecipeIndex(type)` → `ItemID.Sets.IsCrafted[type]`; if < 0 return -1; if crimson and IsCraftedCrimson >= 0 return that; if !crimson and IsCraftedCorruption >=0 return that; else IsCrafted.

	public void GetShimmered()
	{
		int shimmerEquivalentType = inner.GetShimmerEquivalentType();

		int transformToItem = ShimmerTransforms.GetTransformToItem(shimmerEquivalentType);

				stack *= 10000;
				break;

					stack = 1;

				stack *= 1000000;

					WorldItem worldItem = Main.item[num12];
					worldItem.stack = num11;
					worldItem.shimmerTime = 1f;
					worldItem.shimmered = true;
					worldItem.shimmerWet = true;
					worldItem.wet = true;
					worldItem.velocity *= 0.1f;
					worldItem.playerIndexTheItemIsReservedFor = Main.myPlayer;
					if (flag)
					{
						worldItem.velocity.X = 1f * (float)num7;
						worldItem.velocity.X *= 1f + (float)num7 * 0.05f;
						if (num7 % 2 == 0)
						{
							worldItem.velocity.X *= -1f;
						}
					}
					NetMessage.SendData(145, -1, -1, null, num12, 1f);
				}
			}
			stack -= num6 * recipe.createItem.stack;
			if (stack <= 0)
			{
				stack = 0;
				type = 0;
			}
		}
		if (stack > 0)
		{
			shimmerTime = 1f;
		}
		else
		{
			shimmerTime = 0f;
		}
		shimmerWet = true;
		wet = true;
		velocity *= 0.1f;
		if (Main.netMode == 0)
		{
			ShimmerEffect(base.Center);
		}
		else
		{
			NetMessage.SendData(146, -1, -1, null, 0, (int)base.Center.X, (int)base.Center.Y);
			NetMessage.SendData(145, -1, -1, null, whoAmI, 1f);
		}
		AchievementsHelper.NotifyProgressionEvent(27);
		if (stack == 0)
		{
			makeNPC = -1;
			TurnToAir();
		}
	}

	private static void SyncItemsInSection(int toClient, Point sectionCoordinates)
	{
		Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(sectionCoordinates.X * 200 * 16, sectionCoordinates.Y * 150 * 16, 3200, 2400);
		rectangle.Inflate(16, 16);
		for (int i = 0; i < 400; i++)
		{
			WorldItem worldItem = Main.item[i];
			if (worldItem.active && rectangle.Contains(worldItem.Center.ToPoint()))
			{
				NetMessage.SendData(160, toClient, -1, null, i);
			}
		}
	}
}

```

Excellent — that's the full vanilla decraft algorithm. Key points:

1. `num6 = inner.FindDecraftAmount()` = `stack / recipe.createItem.stack` (integer division).
2. For each required item in `recipe.requiredItemQuickLookup` (in order), `num8 = num6 * item.stack`; if `recipe.alchemy`, randomly remove ~1/3 (for each unit, 1/3 chance to decrement). RecipeGroup items → `item.RecipeGroup.DecraftItemId`.
3. Drop stacks capped at 9999 each, `shimmerTime = 1f`, shimmered, wet, velocity damped; if more than 1 required item, spread velocity X by index.
4. `stack -= num6 * recipe.createItem.stack`; if <= 0 → turn to air.
5. Note: `Shimmering()` — check when GetShimmered is called (shimmerTime accumulation). Also note CanShimmer gating.

Note also that `FindDecraftAmount` returns -1 if recipeIndex < 0, but the branch already requires decraftingRecipeIndex >= 0. Also note that the decraft branch runs even if `num6 == 0`? If num6 == 0 → no drops, stack -= 0 → nothing happens. But CanShimmer requires `IsItemDecraftableAndIsDecraftUnlocked` which requires stack/createItem.stack > 0. Good.

125	export function coinLuckAmount(vid: number, stack: number): number {
126	  switch (vid) {
127	    case 72: return stack * 100;
128	    case 73: return stack * 10000;
129	    case 74: return Math.min(stack, 1) * 1000000;
130	    default: return stack; // 71 铜币 ×1

155	/** TryFindingShimmerFreeSpot（Player.cs:28414-28469）净效果版：以 (tx,ty) 为心，
156	 *  半径 r ∈ {1,3,…,59}（原版 num=60 循环步 2）找第一个满足 IsSpotShimmerFree
157	 *  （ShimmerHelper.cs:67-82 三判）的落点：
158	 *  ① 玩家盒 3 格高（y-2..y）无实心无微光（SolidCollision(landing, w, h)）
159	 *  ② 下方 100px（7 格）内有地面（SolidCollision(+height, 100, allowSolidTop)——
160	 *     非紧贴一格；旧实现过严致湖底斜坡/半砖旁找不到）
161	 *  ③ 玩家盒 + 下方 100px 全无微光（WetCollision(landing, w, h+100) && shimmer → 拒）——
162	 *     旧实现漏此判：湖面之上 3 格空气 + 湖面在 7 格内的列被当有效落点 → 传送后
163	 *     落回湖里（用户实测"传送完还在湖里"）
164	 *  **三层兜底**（原版 :28442-28469）：玩家周围失败 → 世界出生点周围 →
165	 *  主出生点（本仓二者同源 spawnX/Y，合并为一层）——保证必能脱困，不会永久卡住 */
166	export function findShimmerFreeSpot(
167	  st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number,
168	  fallbackTx?: number, fallbackTy?: number,
169	): { x: number; y: number } | null {
170	  const clear = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
171	    for (let j = y - 2; j <= y; j++) {
172	      if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return false;
173	      const i = st.idx(x, j);
174	      if (st.isSolid(x, j) || (st.liquid[i] > 0 && st.liquidType[i] === 4)) return false;
175	    }
176	    return true;
177	  };
178	  // 原版第二判：落点下方 100px（约 7 格）内存在 solid 或 solidTop（allowSolidTop=true）
179	  const groundBelow = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
180	    for (let j = y + 1; j <= y + 7; j++) {
181	      if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return false;
182	      if (st.isSolid(x, j) || st.isPlatform(x, j)) return true;
183	    }
184	    return false;
185	  };
186	  // 原版第三判（ShimmerHelper.cs:80-81 WetCollision(landing, w, h+100)）：落点
187	  // 下方 100px 内不得有微光——微光列（如湖面正上方的空气）视为无效落点
188	  const shimmerBelow = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
189	    for (let j = y + 1; j <= y + 7; j++) {
190	      if (!st.inBounds(x, j)) return false;
191	      const i = st.idx(x, j);
192	      if (st.liquid[i] > 0 && st.liquidType[i] === 4) return false;
193	    }
194	    return true;
195	  };
196	  const search = (cx: number, cy: number): { x: number; y: number } | null => {
197	    for (let r = 1; r < 60; r += 2) {
198	      // 切比雪夫半径逐圈外扩（原版 i 循环步 2 语义；原版仅扫环上 8 点/圈，
199	      // 此处全量整数扫描为其超集——命中的落点满足同一谓词，行为相容）
200	      for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
201	        for (let dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
202	          if (Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) < r - 1) continue; // 只扫外环
203	          const x = cx + dx, y = cy + dy;
204	          if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !groundBelow(x, y) || !shimmerBelow(x, y)) continue;
205	          if (clear(x, y)) return { x, y };
206	        }
207	      }
208	    }
209	    return null;
210	  };
211	  return search(tx, ty)
212	    ?? (fallbackTx !== undefined && fallbackTy !== undefined ? search(fallbackTx, fallbackTy) : null);
213	}
214	
215	/** 世界坐标落点 → 玩家传送位（左上角 px 锚，ShimmerHelper.cs:9 + Player.cs:28400-28402）。
216	 *  原版 FindSpotWithoutShimmer 返回 vector2 = (tile*16, tile*16) + (-width/2, -height)——
217	 *  即【玩家盒左上角】坐标（脚底正好贴 tile 底边）；ShimmerUnstuck 再 Teleport(vector+(0,-2))。
218	 *  旧实现 {x: tile*16, y: tile*16-2} 漏掉 -width/2/-height → 盒顶对齐格顶、脚部沉入
219	 *  下方实心 40px = 用户实测"半身在地里"。默认 20/42 = Player 构造尺寸（Player.cs:55083）。 */
220	export function shimmerTeleportPos(
221	  spot: { x: number; y: number }, w = 20, h = 42,
222	): { x: number; y: number } {
223	  return { x: spot.x * TILE - w / 2, y: spot.y * TILE - h - 2 };
224	}
225

151	   *  （netMode!=1 门对单人恒真） */
152	  private updateShimmer(game: GameHooks): void {
153	    const st = game.world.store;
154	    const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE);
155	    // :810-812 采样 (Center.X/16, position.Y/16 - 1)——物品顶上一格
156	    const ty = Math.floor(this.y / TILE) - 1;
157	    const vid = vanillaIdOfItem(this.itemId);
158	    const inShimmer = st.inBounds(tx, ty)
159	      && st.liquid[st.idx(tx, ty)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(tx, ty)] === 4;
160	    if (inShimmer && canShimmerItem(vid, false, game.world.clock.moonPhase)) {
161	      this.shimmerTime += 0.01;
162	      if (this.shimmerTime > 0.9) {
163	        this.shimmerTime = 0.9;
164	        this.getShimmered(game);
165	      }
166	    } else if (this.shimmerTime > 0) {
167	      this.shimmerTime = Math.max(0, this.shimmerTime - 0.01);
168	    }
169	  }

173	   *  560 登记未移植（src/stats/Shimmer.ts GAP 清单） */
174	  private getShimmered(game: GameHooks): void {
175	    const vid = vanillaIdOfItem(this.itemId);
176	    const target = getTransformToItem(vid, game.world.clock.moonPhase);   // 3461 月相砖动态分支
177	    if (vid === 71 || vid === 72 || vid === 73 || vid === 74) {
178	      // 钱币 → 微光化币（:1791-1810）：面额放大后 AddCoinLuck（距离 <1000px 才累计，
179	      // LuckState.addCoinLuck 1:1），物品消失
180	      const amount = coinLuckAmount(vid, this.stack);
181	      const player = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
182	      const dist = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy);
183	      player.luckState.addCoinLuck(dist, amount);
184	      this.stack = 0;
185	      this.dead = true;
186	      return;
187	    }
188	    if (target > 0) {
189	      const iid = internalIdOfVanilla(target);
190	      if (iid >= 0) {
191	        this.itemId = iid;       // SetDefaults(target) 原堆叠保留（:1813-1817）
192	        this.shimmered = true;    // 上浮漂浮
193	        this.shimmerTime = 1;
194	        this.vx *= 0.1; this.vy *= 0.1;
195	        game.playSfx('shimmerSplash', 0.75, this.cx, this.cy);
196	      }
197	    }
198	  }

68	  // RecipeGroup 假 id 聚合（组内所有物品持有数之和）
69	  for (const [gidS, items] of Object.entries(RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS)) {
70	    let sum = 0;
71	    for (const it of items) sum += owned.get(it) ?? 0;
72	    if (sum > 0) add(1000000 + Number(gidS), sum);
73	  }
74	  return owned;
75	}
76	
77	/** 材料匹配（RequiredItemEntry.Matches Recipe.cs:35-42）：普通物比 id，组查假 id */
78	function itemMatches(reqId: number, groupIds: number[], owned: Map<number, number>): number {
79	  if (reqId >= 1000000) return owned.get(reqId) ?? 0;
80	  return owned.get(reqId) ?? 0;
81	}
82	
83	/** 单条配方当前可合成次数（HowManyTimesCanRecipeBeCrafted Recipe.cs:289-310）；
84	 *  返回 0 = 材料不足 */
85	export function craftableCount(r: VanillaRecipe, owned: Map<number, number>): number {
86	  let min = Infinity;
87	  for (const req of r.items) {
88	    const have = itemMatches(req.id, r.groups, owned);
89	    if (have < req.stack) return 0;
90	    min = Math.min(min, Math.floor(have / req.stack));
91	  }
92	  return min === Infinity ? 0 : min;
93	}
94	
95	/** 站点 TileID → 本仓库内部 tile id（TILE_BY_KEY 反查 v_<id>_ key 不稳定，按 vanilla.sheet 扫 TILE_DEFS） */
96	const SHEET_TO_INTERNAL = (() => {
97	  const m = new Map<number, number>();
98	  for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
99	    const sheet = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
100	    if (sheet !== undefined && !m.has(sheet)) m.set(sheet, i);
101	  }
102	  return m;
103	})();
104	
105	export interface VanillaCraftCtx {
106	  /** 附近站点（内部 tile id 集合——Game.stationsNearby 扫描结果） */
107	  nearbyInternal: Set<number>;
108	  /** 附近有炼金台（tile 355/699 经继承算 tile 13；player.alchemyTable）——炼金配方材料 1/3 免费 */
109	  alchemyTable?: boolean;
110	  /** 附近液体：水/蜂蜜/岩浆源（adjWaterSource/adjHoney/adjLava） */
111	  nearWater?: boolean;
112	  nearHoney?: boolean;
113	  nearLava?: boolean;
114	  zoneSnow?: boolean;
115	  zoneGraveyard?: boolean;
116	}
117	
118	/** 环境条件（PlayerMeetsEnvironmentConditions Recipe.cs:321-357） */
119	export function envOk(r: VanillaRecipe, ctx: VanillaCraftCtx): boolean {
120	  if (r.tile >= 0) {
121	    const expanded = expandStationTiles(r.tile);
122	    // 任一等价站点在附近（adjTile[requiredTile] + CountsAs 继承）
123	    const ok = expanded.some((t) => {
124	      const internal = SHEET_TO_INTERNAL.get(t);
125	      return internal !== undefined && ctx.nearbyInternal.has(internal);
126	    });
127	    if (!ok) return false;
128	  }
129	  if (r.water && !ctx.nearWater) return false;
130	  if (r.honey && !ctx.nearHoney) return false;
131	  if (r.lava && !ctx.nearLava) return false;
132	  if (r.snow && !ctx.zoneSnow) return false;
133	  if (r.graveyard && !ctx.zoneGraveyard) return false;
134	  return true;
135	}
136	
137	/** 成品 → 本仓库 item key（vi_<id>_* 反查） */
138	const VID_TO_KEY = (() => {
139	  const m = new Map<number, string>();
140	  for (const def of ITEM_DEFS) {
141	    const vid = def.vid ?? (def.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(def.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
142	    if (vid >= 0 && !m.has(vid)) m.set(vid, def.key);
143	  }
144	  return m;
145	})();
146	
147	export function vanillaItemKey(vid: number): string | undefined {
148	  return VID_TO_KEY.get(vid);
149	}
150	
151	/** 材料原版 id → 显示名用的本仓库 key（组内物品可能部分缺，取第一个存在的） */
152	export function ingredientKey(vid: number): string | undefined {
153	  if (vid >= 1000000) {
154	    const items = RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(vid - 1000000)];
155	    if (items) for (const it of items) {
156	      const k = VID_TO_KEY.get(it);
157	      if (k) return k;
158	    }
159	    return undefined;
160	  }
161	  return VID_TO_KEY.get(vid);
162	}
163	
164	/** 计算可用配方索引列表（UpdateRecipeList 主干） */
165	export function availableRecipes(inv: Inventory, ctx: VanillaCraftCtx): Array<{ r: VanillaRecipe; count: number }> {
166	  const owned = collectOwnedItems(inv);
167	  const out: Array<{ r: VanillaRecipe; count: number }> = [];
168	  for (const r of VANILLA_RECIPES) {
169	    if (!envOk(r, ctx)) continue;
170	    const count = craftableCount(r, owned);
171	    if (count > 0) out.push({ r, count });
172	  }
173	  return out;
174	}
175	
176	/** 执行合成（CraftingRequests.CraftLocally 主干）：扣材料→返成品 key（放置进背包由调用方） */
177	export function consumeMaterials(inv: Inventory, r: VanillaRecipe, times = 1, alchemyTable = false): boolean {
178	  for (const req of r.items) {
179	    let need = req.stack * times;
180	    // 炼金折扣（GetIngredientCraftingDiscount Recipe.cs:213-227：alchemy 配方且 alchemyTable
181	    // 时每个材料独立掷 1/3 概率该份免费——按"每份独立掷骰"逐个扣）
182	    if (r.alchemy && alchemyTable && times === 1) {
183	      let discounted = 0;
184	      for (let k = 0; k < need; k++) if (Math.random() < 1 / 3) discounted++;
185	      need -= discounted;
186	      if (need <= 0) continue;
187	    }
188	    // 组材料：按组内物品依序扣
189	    if (req.id >= 1000000) {
190	      const items = RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(req.id - 1000000)] ?? [];
191	      for (const gid of items) {
192	        if (need <= 0) break;
193	        const key = VID_TO_KEY.get(gid);
194	        if (!key) continue;
195	        const internal = (Object as unknown as { _k?: never });
196	        void internal;
197	        // 直接经 ITEM_DEFS 反查内部 id 再 inv.remove
198	        const iid = invIndexOfVanilla(gid);
199	        if (iid < 0) continue;
200	        const have = inv.countOf(iid);
201	        const take = Math.min(have, need);
202	        if (take > 0) { inv.remove(iid, take); need -= take; }
203	      }
204	      if (need > 0) return false;
205	    } else {
206	      const iid = invIndexOfVanilla(req.id);
207	      if (iid < 0 || inv.countOf(iid) < need) return false;
208	      inv.remove(iid, need);
209	    }
210	  }
211	  return true;
212	}
213	
214	/** 原版 item id → Inventory 内部 item id（缓存） */
215	const _iidCache = new Map<number, number>();
216	export function invIndexOfVanilla(vid: number): number {
217	  let hit = _iidCache.get(vid);
218	  if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
219	  const key = VID_TO_KEY.get(vid);
220	  // ITEM_DEFS 下标即内部 id——线性扫一次后缓存
221	  for (let i = 0; i < ITEM_DEFS.length; i++) {
222	    const d = ITEM_DEFS[i];
223	    const v = d.vid ?? (d.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(d.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
224	    if (v === vid) { hit = i; break; }
225	  }
226	  hit ??= -1;
227	  _iidCache.set(vid, hit);
228	  return hit;
229	}
230	
231	/** 供合成 UI 判定"此物品是合成材料吗"（ItemID.Sets.IsAMaterial 近似：出现在任一配方材料中） */
232	export const MATERIAL_VIDS = (() => {
233	  const s = new Set<number>();
234	  for (const r of VANILLA_RECIPES) for (const req of r.items) s.add(req.id);
235	  return s;
236	})();
237	
238	export type VanillaRecipeLike = VanillaRecipe;
239	export { itemCombat };
240

Implementation in Shimmer.ts canShimmerItem:
```ts
export function canShimmerItem(vid, downedMoonlord=false, moonPhase?, decraft?) {
  if (isItemTransformLocked(vid, downedMoonlord)) return false;
  if (getTransformToItem(vid, moonPhase) > 0 || COMMON_COIN.has(vid)) return true;
  if (decraft) return isItemDecraftable(getShimmerEquivalentType(vid, true), decraft.stack, decraft);
  return false;
}
```

      k++;
      const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[pile.vid];
      if (!key) continue;
      const spread = res.multi; // flag = recipe.requiredItem[1].stack > 0
      let vx = this.vx * 0.1, vy = this.vy * 0.1;
      if (spread) { vx = 1 * k * (1 + k*0.05) * (k % 2 === 0 ? -1 : 1); }
      const d = game.spawnDrop(this.x, this.y, key, pile.stack, vx, vy);
      if (d) { d.shimmered = true; d.shimmerTime = 1; }
    }
    this.stack -= res.consumed;
    if (this.stack <= 0) { this.stack = 0; this.dead = true; }
    else { this.shimmered = true; this.shimmerTime = 1; }

  }
}

Now UpdateWhichItemsAreCrafted semantics with notDecraftable: only recipes with !notDecraftable set IsCrafted. So reverse recipes (notDecraftable) never become decraft sources. 

So in Decraft.ts, when building isCrafted map: skip recipes with notDecraftable flag.

crimson/corruption: `if (recipe.crimson) IsCraftedCrimson[create] = i;` — NO notDecraftable check for these! Look at code:

if (Main.recipe[i].corruption)
    ItemID.Sets.IsCraftedCorruption[...] = i;
```

Yes — crimson/corruption maps set regardless of notDecraftable. Replicate exactly.

Now GetDecraftingRecipeIndex: 
```
num = IsCrafted[type]; if (num < 0) return -1;
if (crimson && IsCraftedCrimson[type] >= 0) return IsCraftedCrimson[type];
if (!crimson && IsCraftedCorruption[type] >= 0) return IsCraftedCorruption[type];
return num;
```

Right — that's the point: decraft in crimson world gives crimson-tier materials.

Now which recipes have crimson/corruption flags — 15 each, likely ingredient-variant pairs.

Now RecipeSets locks: PostSkeletron[recipeIndex] = recipe.ContainsIngredient(154). PostGolem = ContainsIngredient(1101). On the selected decraft recipe index. Note: it's the LOCKED recipe that matters, and since index lookups return actual indices, we just compute per-recipe.

Also the Wiki: "decrafting items that require Bones to craft requires defeating Skeletron; items requiring Lihzahrd Brick require defeating Golem." Consistent.

export const TILE_COUNTS_AS = DOC.tileCountsAs;

/** 站点继承递归展开（Player.SetAdjTile :35192-35208）：tile 本身 + CountsAs 链全部置位 */
// ================= decraft 索引表（Recipe.UpdateWhichItemsAreCrafted :15110-15127 1:1） =================
// 注册序遍历后写 = last-wins（原版同款）; notDecraftable 跳过 IsCrafted;
// crimson/corruption 配方写各自专属表（GetDecraftingRecipeIndex 按世界 evil 取用）。
// 惰性构建一次（模块加载即建,3173 条线性扫）。
const IS_CRAFTED = new Map<number, number>();
const IS_CRAFTED_CRIMSON = new Map<number, number>();
const IS_CRAFTED_CORRUPTION = new Map<number, number>();
{
  const rs = DOC.recipes;
  for (let i = 0; i < rs.length; i++) {
    const r = rs[i];
    if (!r.notDecraftable) IS_CRAFTED.set(r.create, i);
    if (r.crimson) IS_CRAFTED_CRIMSON.set(r.create, i);
    if (r.corruption) IS_CRAFTED_CORRUPTION.set(r.create, i);
  }
}

/** ShimmerTransforms.GetDecraftingRecipeIndex（ShimmerTransforms.cs:15-31）:
 *  IsCrafted<0 → -1;猩红世界取 crimson 表(≥0 才用),腐化世界同理,否则 IsCrafted */
export function getDecraftingRecipeIndex(vid: number, crimsonWorld: boolean): number {
  const base = IS_CRAFTED.get(vid) ?? -1;
  if (base < 0) return -1;
  if (crimsonWorld) {
    const c = IS_CRAFTED_CRIMSON.get(vid) ?? -1;
    if (c >= 0) return c;
  } else {
    const c = IS_CRAFTED_CORRUPTION.get(vid) ?? -1;
    if (c >= 0) return c;
  }
  return base;
}

/** 按索引取配方（decraft 执行端用） */
export function recipeAt(index: number): VanillaRecipe | undefined {
  return DOC.recipes[index];
}

export function expandStationTiles(tile: number): number[] {
  const out = [tile];
  let cur = tile;
  for (let hop = 0; hop < 6; hop++) {
    const next = TILE_COUNTS_AS[String(cur)];
    if (next === undefined) break;
    out.push(next);
    cur = next;
  }
  return out;
}

/** vi_<id> key → 原版 item id 反解 */
export function vanillaIdOfItemKey(key: string): number {
  const m = key.match(/^vi_(\d+)_/);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : -1;
}

/** 玩家持有的原版 id 计数表（含 RecipeGroup 假 id 聚合，Recipe.cs:408-415） */
export function collectOwnedItems(inv: Inventory): Map<number, number> {
  const owned = new Map<number, number>();
  const add = (vid: number, n: number) => owned.set(vid, (owned.get(vid) ?? 0) + n);
  const inc = (key: string, stack: number) => {
    const def = ITEM_DEFS[inv[key as never] as unknown as number];
    void def;
  };
  void inc;
  // slots 0-57（背包+钱币+弹药）
  for (const s of inv.slots) {
    if (!s) continue;
    const def = ITEM_DEFS[s.id];
    if (!def) continue;
    const vid = def.vid ?? (def.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(def.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
    if (vid >= 0) add(vid, s.stack);
  }
  // RecipeGroup 假 id 聚合（组内所有物品持有数之和）
  for (const [gidS, items] of Object.entries(RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS)) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (const it of items) sum += owned.get(it) ?? 0;
    if (sum > 0) add(1000000 + Number(gidS), sum);
  }
  return owned;
}

/** 材料匹配（RequiredItemEntry.Matches Recipe.cs:35-42）：普通物比 id，组查假 id */
function itemMatches(reqId: number, groupIds: number[], owned: Map<number, number>): number {
  if (reqId >= 1000000) return owned.get(reqId) ?? 0;
  return owned.get(reqId) ?? 0;
}

/** 单条配方当前可合成次数（HowManyTimesCanRecipeBeCrafted Recipe.cs:289-310）；
 *  返回 0 = 材料不足 */
export function craftableCount(r: VanillaRecipe, owned: Map<number, number>): number {
  let min = Infinity;
  for (const req of r.items) {
    const have = itemMatches(req.id, r.groups, owned);
    if (have < req.stack) return 0;
    min = Math.min(min, Math.floor(have / req.stack));
  }
  return min === Infinity ? 0 : min;
}

/** ShimmerTransforms.IsItemDecraftableAndIsDecraftUnlocked（:64-80）：
 *  等价物(forDecrafting)有配方 + 未锁 + stack ≥ createStack */
export function isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(
  vid: number, stack: number,
  crimsonWorld: boolean, downedBoss3: boolean, downedGolemBoss: boolean,
): boolean {
  const eq = getShimmerEquivalentType(vid, true);
  const idx = getDecraftingRecipeIndex(eq, crimsonWorld);
  if (isRecipeIndexDecraftLocked(idx, downedBoss3, downedGolemBoss)) return false;
  if (idx < 0) return false;
  const r = recipeAt(idx)!;
  return Math.floor(stack / r.createStack) > 0;
}

/** Item.FindDecraftAmount（Item.cs:49063-49071）：stack / createStack(向下取整) */
export function findDecraftAmount(vid: number, stack: number, crimsonWorld: boolean): number {
  const idx = getDecraftingRecipeIndex(getShimmerEquivalentType(vid, true), crimsonWorld);
  if (idx < 0) return -1;
  return Math.floor(stack / recipeAt(idx)!.createStack);
}

/** RecipeGroup.DecraftItemId（RecipeGroup.cs:59）：组内**价值最低**物品代表。
 *  vanilla-itemvalue.json 提供原版 value 表,OrderBy(value).First() 1:1 */
const GROUP_DECRAFT_ID = new Map<number, number>();
export function groupDecraftItemId(groupId: number): number {
  const hit = GROUP_DECRAFT_ID.get(groupId);
  if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
  const items = RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(groupId)] ?? [];
  let best = items[0] ?? 0;
  let bestV = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  for (const it of items) {
    const v = (itemValueJson as Record<string, number>)[String(it)] ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
    if (v < bestV) { bestV = v; best = it; }
  }
  GROUP_DECRAFT_ID.set(groupId, best);
  return best;
}

/** Item.CanShimmer（Item.cs:49045-49068）2026-08-13 补 decraft 位（:49056）：
 *  可转 = 未锁 + (有转化目标 ∨ **可分解且未锁** ∨ 钱币)。
 *  decraftCtx 缺省 = 不启用 decraft 判定（调用方旧签名兼容） */
export function canShimmerItem(
  vid: number, downedMoonlord = false, moonPhase?: number,
  decraftCtx?: { stack: number; crimsonWorld: boolean; downedBoss3: boolean; downedGolemBoss: boolean },
): boolean {
  if (isItemTransformLocked(vid, downedMoonlord)) return false;
  if (getTransformToItem(vid, moonPhase) > 0 || COMMON_COIN.has(vid)) return true;
  if (decraftCtx) return isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(vid, decraftCtx.stack, decraftCtx.crimsonWorld, decraftCtx.downedBoss3, decraftCtx.downedGolemBoss);
  return false;
}

/** 钱币→微光化币面额放大（WorldItem.cs:1791-1810）：铜 ×1 / 银 ×100 / 金 ×10000 /
 *  铂 stack 钳 1 后 ×1000000。返回入 coinLuck 的铜币面额（stack 一并放大） */
export function coinLuckAmount(vid: number, stack: number): number {
  switch (vid) {
    case 72: return stack * 100;
    case 73: return stack * 10000;
    case 74: return Math.min(stack, 1) * 1000000;
    default: return stack; // 71 铜币 ×1
  }
}

// ============ NPC 侧（NPC.GetShimmered，NPC.cs:92502-92584） ============

/** NPCID.Sets.ShimmerTransformToNPC（→ NPC.Transform 保持实体） */
export function npcShimmerTransformTo(npcId: number): number {
  const t = NPC_TRANSFORM[npcId];
  return t !== undefined && t >= 0 ? t : -1;
}

/** NPCID.Sets.ShimmerTransformToItem（→ 掉微光化物品后消散） */
export function npcShimmerTransformToItem(npcId: number): number {
  const t = NPC_TRANSFORM_ITEM[npcId];
  return t !== undefined && t >= 0 ? t : -1;
}

/** NPCID.Sets.ShimmerTownTransform（城镇 NPC 微光变体，ai[0]=25） */
export function isNpcTownShimmerable(npcId: number): boolean {
  return NPC_TOWN_TRANSFORM.has(npcId);
}

// ============ 玩家脱困传送（ShimmerHelper.FindSpotWithoutShimmer 近似） ============

 *     旧实现漏此判：湖面之上 3 格空气 + 湖面在 7 格内的列被当有效落点 → 传送后
 *     落回湖里（用户实测"传送完还在湖里"）
 *  **三层兜底**（原版 :28442-28469）：玩家周围失败 → 世界出生点周围 →
 *  主出生点（本仓二者同源 spawnX/Y，合并为一层）——保证必能脱困，不会永久卡住 */
export function findShimmerFreeSpot(
  st: TileStore, tx: number, ty: number,

172:   *  接通）；ShimmerTransformToItem → 换为目标物品原堆叠。decraft/makeNPC/特例 4986、
---

  /** WorldItem.Shimmering（:806-840）：脚上方格是微光且可转化 → 进度 +0.01/t，
   *  >0.9 触发 GetShimmered；不在微光中则进度衰减。转化触发在本地玩家侧语义
   *  （netMode!=1 门对单人恒真） */
  private updateShimmer(game: GameHooks): void {
    const st = game.world.store;
    const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE);
    // :810-812 采样 (Center.X/16, position.Y/16 - 1)——物品顶上一格
    const ty = Math.floor(this.y / TILE) - 1;
    const vid = vanillaIdOfItem(this.itemId);
    const inShimmer = st.inBounds(tx, ty)
      && st.liquid[st.idx(tx, ty)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(tx, ty)] === 4;
    if (inShimmer && canShimmerItem(vid, false, game.world.clock.moonPhase)) {
      this.shimmerTime += 0.01;
      if (this.shimmerTime > 0.9) {
        this.shimmerTime = 0.9;
        this.getShimmered(game);
      }
    } else if (this.shimmerTime > 0) {
      this.shimmerTime = Math.max(0, this.shimmerTime - 0.01);
    }
  }

  /** WorldItem.GetShimmered（:1781-1931）本仓子集：钱币 → coinLuck（Luck.ts 数值位
   *  接通）；ShimmerTransformToItem → 换为目标物品原堆叠。decraft/makeNPC/特例 4986、
   *  560 登记未移植（src/stats/Shimmer.ts GAP 清单） */
  private getShimmered(game: GameHooks): void {
    const vid = vanillaIdOfItem(this.itemId);
    const target = getTransformToItem(vid, game.world.clock.moonPhase);   // 3461 月相砖动态分支
    if (vid === 71 || vid === 72 || vid === 73 || vid === 74) {
      // 钱币 → 微光化币（:1791-1810）：面额放大后 AddCoinLuck（距离 <1000px 才累计，
      // LuckState.addCoinLuck 1:1），物品消失
      const amount = coinLuckAmount(vid, this.stack);
      const player = (game as unknown as { player: Player }).player;
      const dist = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy);
      player.luckState.addCoinLuck(dist, amount);
      this.stack = 0;
      this.dead = true;
      return;
    }
    if (target > 0) {
      const iid = internalIdOfVanilla(target);
      if (iid >= 0) {
        this.itemId = iid;       // SetDefaults(target) 原堆叠保留（:1813-1817）
        this.shimmered = true;    // 上浮漂浮
        this.shimmerTime = 1;
        this.vx *= 0.1; this.vy *= 0.1;
        game.playSfx('shimmerSplash', 0.75, this.cx, this.cy);
      }
    }
  }

  inWater = false;

  draw() { /* Renderer 统一绘制 */ }
}

  private updateShimmer(game: GameHooks): void {

Hmm — decraftOutcome doesn't check locks. isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked does. Simplest: gate with isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(vid, stack, crimson, boss3, golem) then compute outcome. Two lookups but clean and 1:1 (CanShimmer already gates too). Let me write:

```ts
private tryDecraft(game: GameHooks, vid: number): boolean {
  const flags = ...;
  const crimsonWorld = !!game.world.crimson;
  const downedBoss3 = !!(flags['downed_35'] || flags.downedSkeletron);
  const downedGolemBoss = !!flags['downed_245'];
  // :1878 入口条件：配方存在且未锁且 stack ≥ createStack（CanShimmer 同谓词）
  if (!isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(vid, this.stack, crimsonWorld, downedBoss3, downedGolemBoss)) return false;
  const outcome = decraftOutcome(vid, this.stack, crimsonWorld);
  if (!outcome || !outcome.piles.length) return false;
  outcome.piles.forEach((p, k) => {
    const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[p.vid];
    if (!key) return;
    const n = k + 1;   // 原版 num7 从 1 起（:1881/1929-1936）
    let vx = this.vx * 0.1;
    if (outcome.spread) vx = n * (1 + n * 0.05) * (n % 2 === 0 ? -1 : 1);
    const d = game.spawnDrop(this.cx, this.cy, key, p.stack, vx, this.vy * 0.1);
    if (!d) return;
    d.shimmered = true;   // :1924 上浮漂浮
    d.shimmerTime = 1;    // :1923
  });
  this.stack -= outcome.consumed;         // :1927-1933
  if (this.stack <= 0) { this.stack = 0; this.dead = true; }
  else this.shimmerTime = 1;             // :1936 剩余 stack 重置浸入进度（原版同位语义）
  game.playSfx('shimmerSplash', 0.75, this.cx, this.cy);
  return true;
}
```

And reorder getShimmered: coin → transform → decraft (vanilla order; skipping 4986/560/makeNPC GAP).

+lit[1] : null;\n};\nwhile ((m = caseRe.exec(src))) {\n  const id = +m[1];\n  const body = m[2].slice(0, 1200);\n  const r = body.match(/\\brare = (-?\\d+);/);\n  const v = body.match(/\\bvalue = ([^;]+);/);\n  if (r) RARE[id] = +r[1];\n  if (v) {\n    const val = evalValue(v[1].trim());\n    if (val !== null) VALUE[id] = val;\n  }\n  SEGMENTS.push({ id, hasRare: !!r, hasValue: VALUE[id] !== undefined });\n}\n// fallthrough 继承：空体段落入源码序下一非空段的语句（跨段直至有赋值为止）\nfor (let i = 0; i < SEGMENTS.length; i++) {\n  const seg = SEGMENTS[i];\n  if (seg.hasRare && seg.hasValue) continue;\n  for (let j = i + 1; j < SEGMENTS.length; j++) {\n    const nxt = SEGMENTS[j];\n    if (!seg.hasRare && RARE[nxt.id] !== undefined) { RARE[seg.id] = RARE[nxt.id]; seg.hasRare = true; }\n    if (!seg.hasValue && VALUE[nxt.id] !== undefined) { VALUE[seg.id] = VALUE[nxt.id]; seg.hasValue = true; }\n    if (seg.hasRare && seg.hasValue) break;\n    // 下一段也无赋值（同为空体）→ 继续向后找（链式 fallthrough）\n  }\n}\nwriteFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-itemrare.json`, JSON.stringify(RARE));\nwriteFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-itemvalue.json`, JSON.stringify(VALUE));\nconsole.error(`rare 条目: ${Object.keys(RARE).length} / value 条目: ${Object.keys(VALUE).length}`);\n"

n+=1\n        if n>=6: break\nprint('--- crimson/corruption recipes:')\nfor i,r in enumerate(rec):\n    if r.get('crimson') or r.get('corruption'):\n        print(' ', r['create'], 'crimson' if r.get('crimson') else 'corruption', r['items'], 'transform', tr.get(str(r['create'])))\nprint('--- custom shimmer recipes:')\nfor r in rec:\n    if r.get('shimmer'): print(' ', r['create'], r['items'], '->', r['shimmer'])\nprint('--- countsAsDecraft sample:', dict(list(counts.items())[:8]))\nEOF",

true)!.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 1330, stack: 8 }]);\n  });\n\n  it('组材料【替换】为组代表而非追加（RecipeGroup.DecraftItemId，WorldItem.cs:1895-1897）', () => {\n    // 铁斧 10 = 铁锭×8 + 木头×3（Wood 组成员 9 → 组代表仍是木头 9）。\n    // 旧实现曾把组代表当【额外】一垛追加 → 多返 1 木头，此断言锁定替换语义。\n    const out = decraftOutcome(10, 1, false)!;\n    expect(out.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 22, stack: 8 }, { vid: 9, stack: 3 }]);\n    expect(out.spread).toBe(true);   // ≥2 材料 → 掉落物按序号散射（:1882）\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('数量与拆垛（Item.FindDecraftAmount :49062-49070 / :1913-1916）', () => {\n  it('骨头平台 634 createStack=2：5 个 → 2 次 → 2 骨头，消耗 4；不足一组 → null', () => {\n    expect(decraftOutcome(634, 5, false, () => 2)).toEqual({\n      piles: [{ vid: 154, stack: 2 }], consumed: 4, spread: false,\n    });\n    expect(decraftOutcome(634, 1, false, () => 2)).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it('单垛上限 9999：30000 骨头平台 → 15000 骨头拆 9999+5001 两垛', () => {\n    const out = decraftOutcome(634, 30000, false, () => 2)!;\n    expect(out.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 154, stack: 9999 }, { vid: 154, stack: 5001 }]);\n    expect(out.consumed).toBe(30000);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('RecipeSets 双锁（UpdateRecipeSets :78-84 + IsRecipeIndexDecraftLocked :46-61）', () => {\n  it('骷髅头灯笼 347（含骨头 154）：未败骷髅王锁死，败后返还 骨头×10+火炬×1', () => {\n    expect(isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(347, 1, false, false, true)).toBe(false);\n    expect(canShimmerItem(347, false, undefined, open(1, { downedBoss3: false }))).toBe(false);\n    expect(isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(347, 1, false, true, true)).toBe(true);\n    expect(canShimmerItem(347, false, undefined, open(1))).toBe(true);\n    expect(decraftOutcome(347, 1, false)!.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 154, stack: 10 }, { vid: 8, stack: 1 }]);\n  });\n\n  it('丛林蜥蜴椅 1143（含丛林蜥蜴砖 1101）：未败石巨人锁死，败后返还 砖×4', () => {\n    expect(isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(1143, 1, false, true, false)).toBe(false);\n    expect(isItemDecraftableAndUnlocked(1143, 1, false, true, true)).toBe(true);\n    expect(decraftOutcome(1143, 1, false)!.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 1101, stack: 4 }]);\n  });\n\n  it('锁只看材料表：154/1101 不在任何 RecipeGroup 内（ContainsIngredient 字面比对等价）', () => {\n    for (const items of Object.values(RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS)) {\n      expect((items as number[]).includes(154)).toBe(false);\n      expect((items as number[]).includes(1101)).toBe(false);\n    }\n    expect(isRecipeIndexDecraftLocked(-1, false, false)).toBe(false); // 负索引不锁\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('返还表变体（WorldItem.cs:1884-1911）', () => {\n  it('AddCustomShimmerResult 覆盖：银弹 278（70/组）→ 火枪弹 97 + 银矿石 14（非材料银锭）', () => {\n    const out = decraftOutcome(278, 140, false)!;   // 2 组 → ×2\n    expect(out.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 97, stack: 140 }, { vid: 14, stack: 2 }]);\n    expect(out.consumed).toBe(140);\n    expect(decraftOutcome(278, 69, false)).toBeNull();  // 不足 70 不分解\n  });\n\n  it('铜镐 3509 覆盖表 → 铜矿石 12 + 木头 9（替代 铜锭×8+木头×4 材料表）', () => {\n    expect(decraftOutcome(3509, 1, false)!.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 12, stack: 1 }, { vid: 9, stack: 1 }]);\n  });\n\n  it('炼金配方逐单位 1/3 蒸发：战斗药水 300 无蒸发全返 / 全蒸发空返但仍消耗', () => {\n    // rand3 语义 = Main.rand.Next(3)；==0 蒸发。腐化世界战斗药水 = 瓶装水+死亡草+腐烂块\n    const full = decraftOutcome(300, 1, false, () => 2)!;\n    expect(full.piles).toEqual([{ vid: 126, stack: 1 }, { vid: 316, stack: 1 }, { vid: 68, stack: 1 }]);\n    const gone = decraftOutcome(300, 1, false, () => 0)!;\n    expect(gone.piles).toEqual([]);\n    expect(gone.consumed).toBe(1);   // 原版照扣（:1927），不因空垛回退\n  });\n\n  it('forDecrafting 等价计数：真铜短剑 5669 按 Zenith 4956 配方反分解（10 材料）', () => {\n    const out = decraftOutcome(5669, 1, false)!;\n    expect(out.piles.length).toBe(10);\n    expect(out.piles.map((p) => p.vid)).toContain(757);   // 泰拉刃\n    expect(out.consumed).toBe(1);\n  });\n});\n\n// ============ 端到端（ItemDrop 入微光池 → GetShimmered 分支顺序） ============\n\nconst W = 60, H = 60;\n\nfunction shimmerWorld(): World {\n  const w = new World(W, H, 11, 'decraft-test');\n  const st = w.store;\n  for (let x = 10; x < 30; x++) {\n    st.setTile(x, 40, 1);\n    for (let y = 33; y < 40; y++) st.setLiquid(x, y, 255, 4);\n  }\n  w.lavaLine = 50;\n  return w;\n}\n\ninterface Spawned { key: string; count: number; drop: ItemDrop }\n\nfunction hooks(w: World, p: Player, spawned: Spawned[]): GameHooks {\n  return {\n    world: w, player: p,\n    enemies: () => [], critters: () => [],\n    spawnDrop: (x, y, key, count) => {\n      const d = new ItemDrop(x, y, ITEM_BY_KEY[key], count ?? 1);\n      spawned.push({ key, count: count ?? 1, drop: d });\n      return d;\n    },\n    damagePlayer: () => {}, addDamageNumber: () => {}, cutTile: () => {},\n    onEnemyKilled: () => {}, spawnEnemy: () => {},\n    spawnParticles: () => {}, notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},\n    playSfx: () => {}, playSfxFiles: () => {}, showPickupLabel: () => {},\n  } as unknown as GameHooks;\n}\n\ndescribe('掉落物端到端（分支顺序 ：1790 钱币 → :1812 转化 → :1878 decraft）', () => {\n  it('木桌 32（木头×8）入池 → 分解出 8 木头，原物消失', () => {\n    const w = shimmerWorld();\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const spawned: Spawned[] = [];\n    const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[32];\n    expect(key).toBeDefined();\n    const d = new ItemDrop(20 * TILE, 35 * TILE, ITEM_BY_KEY[key!], 1);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 400 && !d.dead; i++) d.fixedUpdate(1, hooks(w, p, spawned));\n    expect(d.dead).toBe(true);\n    expect(spawned).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[9] && spawned[0].key === VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[9]).toBe(true);\n    expect(spawned[0].count).toBe(8);\n    expect(spawned[0].drop.shimmered).toBe(true);     // 返还物上浮漂浮（:1924）\n    expect(spawned[0].drop.shimmerTime).toBe(1);      // :1923\n  });\n\n  it('火炬 8 同时具备配方与转化目标 → 必走转化（5353），不得 decraft 成 凝胶+木头', () => {\n    const w = shimmerWorld();\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const spawned: Spawned[] = [];\n    const d = new ItemDrop(20 * TILE, 35 * TILE, ITEM_BY_KEY['torch'], 5);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) {\n      d.fixedUpdate(1, hooks(w, p, spawned));\n      if (d.shimmered) break;\n    }\n    expect(d.shimmered).toBe(true);\n    expect(d.stack).toBe(5);\n    expect(spawned).toHaveLength(0);   // 转化不产生返还垛（decraft 抢先 = 回归形态）\n  });\n\n  it('金币 73 有币币兑换配方 → 必走 coinLuck（×10000），不得 decraft 成铂金币', () => {\n    const w = shimmerWorld();\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const spawned: Spawned[] = [];\n    const d = new ItemDrop(20 * TILE, 35 * TILE, ITEM_BY_KEY['coin_gold'], 2);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 200 && !d.dead; i++) d.fixedUpdate(1, hooks(w, p, spawned));\n    expect(d.dead).toBe(true);\n    expect(spawned).toHaveLength(0);\n    expect(p.luckState.coinLuck).toBe(2 * 10000);\n  });\n\n  it('锁门态：骷髅头灯笼 347 在未败骷髅王世界入池不分解（shimmerTime 归零衰减）', () => {\n    const w = shimmerWorld();\n    const p = new Player(45 * TILE, 30 * TILE, new Inventory());\n    const spawned: Spawned[] = [];\n    const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[347];\n    const d = new ItemDrop(20 * TILE, 35 * TILE, ITEM_BY_KEY[key!], 1);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 400; i++) d.fixedUpdate(1, hooks(w, p, spawned));\n    expect(d.dead).toBe(false);\n    expect(d.shimmered).toBe(false);\n    expect(d.shimmerTime).toBe(0);\n    expect(spawned).toHaveLength(0);\n    // 败骷髅王后同池同物 → 分解出 骨头×10+火炬×1 两垛\n    w.flags['downed_35'] = true;\n    const d2 = new ItemDrop(20 * TILE, 35 * TILE, ITEM_BY_KEY[key!], 1);\n    const spawned2: Spawned[] = [];\n    for (let i = 0; i < 400 && !d2.dead; i++) d2.fixedUpdate(1, hooks(w, p, spawned2));\n    expect(d2.dead).toBe(true);\n    expect(spawned2).toHaveLength(2);\n  });\n});\n"
